Classification logic\n    motion_labels = []\n\n    # Check for zoom\n    if scale > 1.1:\n        motion_labels.append(\"Dolly In\")\n    elif scale < 0.9:\n        motion_labels.append(\"Dolly Out\")\n\n    # Threshold to get binary mask\n    _, binary_mask = cv2.threshold(frame_diff, 25, 255, cv2.THRESH_BINARY)\n\n    # Apply morphological operations to clean up\n    kernel = np.ones((5, 5), np.uint8)\n    binary_mask = cv2.morphologyEx(binary_mask, cv2.MORPH_OPEN, kernel)\n    binary_mask = cv2.morphologyEx(binary_mask, cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, kernel)\n\n    # Create CSR representation\n    csr = csr_matrix(mask_bool)\n\n    return {\n        'data': csr.data,\n        'indices': csr.indices,\n        'indptr': csr.indptr,\n        'shape': [H, W]\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    video_path = \"/root/input.mp4\"\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"VIDEO ANALYSIS: Camera Motion & Dynamic Objects\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Threshold to get binary mask\n    _, binary_mask = cv2.threshold(frame_diff, 25, 255, cv2.THRESH_BINARY)\n\n    # Apply morphological operations to clean up\n    kernel = np.ones((5, 5), np.uint8)\n    binary_mask = cv2.morphologyEx(binary_mask, cv2.MORPH_OPEN, kernel)\n    binary_mask = cv2.morphologyEx(binary_mask, cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, kernel)\n\n    # Convert to boolean\n    binary_mask = binary_mask > 0\n\n    return binary_mask\n\ndef dense_to_csr_sparse(mask):\n    \"\"\"Convert dense binary mask to CSR sparse format.\"\"\"\n    H, W = mask.shape\n    mask_bool = mask.astype(bool)\n\n    # Create CSR representation\n    csr = csr_matrix(mask_bool)\n\n    return {\n        'data': csr.data,\n        'indices': csr.indices,\n        'indptr': csr.indptr,\n        'shape': [H, W]\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    video_path = \"/root/input.mp4\"\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"VIDEO ANALYSIS: Camera Motion & Dynamic Objects\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Add shape to masks_data\n    masks_data['shape'] = shape\n\n    for i in range(len(frames) - 1):\n        print(f\"  Processing frame {i} -> {i+1} (sample_ids: {sample_ids[i]} -> {sample_ids[i+1]})\")\n\n        prev_frame = frames[i]\n        curr_frame = frames[i + 1]\n\n        # Convert to grayscale for optical flow\n        prev_gray = cv2.cvtColor(prev_frame, cv2.COLOR_RGB2GRAY)\n        curr_gray = cv2.cvtColor(curr_frame, cv2.COLOR_RGB2GRAY)\n\n        # Estimate camera motion\n        motion = estimate_camera_motion(prev_gray, curr_gray)\n\n        # Classify motion\n        labels = classify_motion(motion)\n        motion_labels[f\"{sample_ids[i]}->{sample_ids[i+1]}\"] = labels\n\n        # Detect dynamic objects\n        dynamic_mask = detect_dynamic_objects(prev_frame, curr_frame, motion)\n\n        # Convert to CSR sparse format\n        csr_sparse = dense_to_csr_sparse(dynamic_mask)\n\n        # Store in format f_{i}_data, f_{i}_indices, f_{i}_indptr\n        frame_key = str(sample_ids[i])\n        masks_data[f'f_{frame_key}_data'] = csr_sparse['data']\n        masks_data[f'f_{frame_key}_indices'] = csr_sparse['indices']\n        masks_data[f'f_{frame_key}_indptr'] = csr_sparse['indptr']\n\n    #
